   ### Development
   
  -All development dependencies (bun, node, typst, similarity, just, etc.) are managed by **devenv** (`devenv.nix`). Run `devenv shell` to enter the dev environment, then use **just** as the task runner.
  +All development dependencies (bun, node, typst, similarity, etc.) are managed by **devenv** (`devenv.nix`). Run `devenv shell` to enter the dev environment.
  +
  +Every build, test, and release workflow is a **devenv task**, namespaced with `keating:`:
  +
  +```bash
  +devenv tasks list          # List all available tasks
  +devenv tasks run keating:test
  +devenv tasks run keating:web
  +```
  +
  +The full task reference lives in [docs/DEVELOPMENT.md](docs/DEVELOPMENT.md); the [devenv tasks documentation](https://devenv.sh/tasks/) covers how tasks work, arguments, and dependencies.

  +function ContextModelSelectorDialog({
  +	open,

  +export interface ImageGenerationModelSelectorDialogProps {
  +	open: boolean;
  +	generator: ImageGeneratorOption;
  +	currentModelId: string;
  +	onClose: () => void;
  +	onSelect: (modelId: string) => void;
  +}
  +
  +/** Only image-generation endpoint models are offered in this context. */
  +export function ImageGenerationModelSelectorDialog({
  +	open,
  +	generator,
  +	currentModelId,
  +	onClose,
  +	onSelect,
  +}: ImageGenerationModelSelectorDialogProps) {
  +	const options = useMemo(
  +		() => generator.models.map((model) => ({ value: model, label: model })),
  +		[generator.models],
  +	);
  +	return (
  +		<ContextModelSelectorDialog
  +			open={open}
  +			title="Select image model"
  +			description={`Only image-generation models from ${generator.label} are shown.`}
  +			searchLabel="Search image models"
  +			emptyLabel="No image-generation models matched this search."
  +			badge="Image generation"
  +			actionLabel="Use image model"
  +			options={options}
  +			currentModelId={currentModelId}
  +			onClose={onClose}
  +			onSelect={onSelect}
  +		/>
  +	);

  +
  +/** Only models compatible with the active speech provider are offered. */
  +export function AudioModelSelectorDialog({
  +	open,
  +	provider,
  +	currentModelId,
  +	onClose,
  +	onSelect,
  +}: AudioModelSelectorDialogProps) {
  +	const isRealtime = provider.kind === "duplex";
  +	return (
  +		<ContextModelSelectorDialog
  +			open={open}
  +			title={isRealtime ? "Select realtime voice model" : "Select speech model"}
  +			description={`Only ${isRealtime ? "realtime voice" : "speech synthesis"} models from ${provider.label} are shown.`}
  +			searchLabel={isRealtime ? "Search realtime voice models" : "Search speech models"}
  +			emptyLabel={`No ${isRealtime ? "realtime voice" : "speech synthesis"} models matched this search.`}
  +			badge={isRealtime ? "Realtime voice" : "Speech output"}
  +			actionLabel="Use audio model"
  +			options={provider.models}
  +			currentModelId={currentModelId}
  +			onClose={onClose}
  +			onSelect={onSelect}
  +		/>
  +	);

improvementArchiveToMarkdown;\nexports.diagnoseBenchmark = diagnoseBenchmark;\nexports.computeTopicEngagement = computeTopicEngagement;\nexports.buildEngagementTimeline = dueTopicsToMarkdown;\nexports.resolveQuizLimits = resolveQuizLimits;\nexports.buildAuthoredQuestions = buildAuthoredQuestions;\nexports.generateQuiz = generateQuiz;\nexports.quizToMarkdown = quizToMarkdown;\nexports.quizAnswerKeyToMarkdown = quizAnswerKeyToMarkdown;\n// ============================================================================\n// Utilities\n// ============================================================================\nfunction clamp(value, min = 0, max = 1) {\n    return Math.max(min, Math.min(max, value));\n}\nfunction mean(values) {\n    if (values.length === 0)\n        return 0;\n    return values.reduce((sum, v) => sum + v, 0) / values.length;\n}\nfunction slugify(text) {\n    return text\n        .toLowerCase()\n        .replace(/[^a-z0-9]+/g, \"-\")\n        .replace(/^-|-$/g, \"\");\n}\nfunction titleCase(text) {\n    return text\n        .split(/\\s+/)\n        .map((word) => word.charAt(0).toUpperCase() + word.slice(1).toLowerCase())\n        .join(\" \");\n}\n// ============================================================================\n// Benchmark Real Outcomes (ported from src/core/benchmark-real.ts)\n// ============================================================================\nexports.MIN_REAL_OUTCOMES = 5;\nfunction feedbackToOutcomeScore(signal) {\n    switch (signal) {\n        case \"thumbs-up\": return 0.85;\n        case \"thumbs-down\": return 0.15;\n        case \"confused\": return 0.35;\n    }\n}\nfunction hasEnoughRealData(outcomes) {\n    return outcomes.length >= exports.MIN_REAL_OUTCOMES;\n}\nfunction blendRealSyntheticScore(realScore, syntheticMean, realOutcomeCount) {\n    const realShare = clamp(0.5 + Math.min(0.4, realOutcomeCount / 20), 0.5, 0.9);\n    const syntheticShare = 1 - realShare;\n    return clamp(realScore * realShare + syntheticMean * syntheticShare, 0, 1);\n}\nfunction computeRealOutcomeScore(outcomes, policy, topic, weights) {\n    const defaultLearner = {\n        id: \"real-learner\",\n        priorKnowledge: 0.5,\n        abstractionComfort: 0.5,\n        analogyNeed: 0.5,\n        dialoguePreference: 0.5,\n        diagramAffinity: 0.5,\n        persistence: 0.5,\n        transferDesire: 0.5,\n        anxiety: 0.3,\n    };\n    const avgOutcome = mean(outcomes.map((outcome) => outcome.outcomeScore));\n    const upRatio = outcomes.filter((outcome) => outcome.feedbackSignal === \"thumbs-up\").length /\n        outcomes.length;\n    const confusedRatio = outcomes.filter((outcome) => outcome.feedbackSignal === \"confused\").length /\n        outcomes.length;\n    const downRatio = outcomes.filter((outcome) => outcome.feedbackSignal === \"thumbs-down\").length /\n        outcomes.length;\n    const avgMastery = mean(outcomes.map((outcome) => outcome.masteryEstimate));\n    const quizScores = outcomes\n        .map((outcome) => outcome.quizScore)\n        .filter((value) => typeof value === \"number\" && !Number.isNaN(value));\n    const avgQuiz = quizScores.length > 0 ? mean(quizScores) : null;\n    // Graded quiz results are direct evidence of mastery, so when present they\n    // dominate the inferred feedback/mastery signals.\n    const masteryGain = avgQuiz === null\n        ? clamp(avgOutcome * 0.6 + avgMastery * 0.4)\n        : clamp(avgQuiz * 0.5 + avgOutcome * 0.3 + avgMastery * 0.2);\n    const retention = clamp(masteryGain * (0.55 + policy.retrievalPractice * 0.45));\n    const engagement = clamp(avgOutcome * 0.7 + upRatio * 0.3);\n    const transfer = clamp(retention * (0.55 + policy.interdisciplinaryBias * 0.25 + avgMastery * 0.2));\n    const confusion = clamp(confusedRatio * 0.6 + downRatio * 0.4);\n    const score = clamp(masteryGain * weights.masteryGain +\n        retention * weights.retention +\n        engagement * weights.engagement +\n        transfer * weights.transfer -\n        confusion * weights.confusion, 0, 1);\n    const explanations = [];\n    if (upRatio > 0.6)\n        explanations.push(\"learner gave mostly positive feedback\");\n    if (confusedRatio > 0.3)\n        explanations.push(\"learner was frequently confused\");\n    if (downRatio > 0.2)\n        explanations.push(\"learner gave substantial negative feedback\");\n    if (avgMastery > 0.7)\n        explanations.push(\"mastery estimates are high\");\n    if (avgMastery < 0.3)\n        explanations.push(\"mastery estimates are low\");\n    if (avgQuiz !== null) {\n        explanations.push(`graded quiz average is ${(avgQuiz * 100).toFixed(0)}% across ${quizScores.length} quiz(zes)`);\n    }\n    if (explanations.length === 0)\n        explanations.push(\"learner feedback is mixed\");\n    return {\n        learner: defaultLearner,\n        topic,\n        masteryGain,\n        retention,\n        engagement,\n        transfer,\n        confusion,\n        score,\n        breakdown: {\n            intuitionFit: avgOutcome,\n            rigorFit: avgOutcome * policy.formalism,\n            dialogueFit: avgOutcome * policy.socraticRatio,\n            diagramFit: avgOutcome * policy.diagramBias,\n            practiceFit: clamp(upRatio * policy.exerciseCount / 5),\n            reflectionFit: avgOutcome * policy.reflectionBias,\n            overload: confusion,\n        },\n        explanation: [\"Real learner outcome (N=\" + outcomes.length + \").\", ...explanations],\n    };\n}\nfunction simulateDeterministicTeaching(policy, topic, learner, weights) {\n    const intuitionFit = 1 - Math.abs(policy.analogyDensity - learner.analogyNeed);\n    const rigorTarget = clamp((topic.formalism + learner.abstractionComfort) / 2);\n    const rigorFit = 1 - Math.abs(policy.formalism - rigorTarget);\n    const dialogueFit = 1 - Math.abs(policy.socraticRatio - learner.dialoguePreference);\n    const diagramTarget = topic.visualizable ? learner.diagramAffinity : 0.2;\n    const diagramFit = 1 - Math.abs(policy.diagramBias - diagramTarget);\n    const practiceNeed = clamp(1 - learner.priorKnowledge + learner.anxiety * 0.2);\n    const practiceFit = 1 - Math.abs(policy.exerciseCount / 5 - practiceNeed);\n    const reflectionFit = 1 - Math.abs(policy.reflectionBias - learner.transferDesire);\n    const overload = clamp(policy.formalism * 0.35 +\n        (policy.exerciseCount / 5) * 0.15 +\n        policy.challengeRate * 0.3 -\n        learner.persistence * 0.2 +\n        learner.anxiety * 0.25 -\n        learner.priorKnowledge * 0.15);\n    const masteryGain = clamp(0.14 +\n        intuitionFit * 0.18 +\n        rigorFit * 0.2 +\n        dialogueFit * 0.12 +\n        diagramFit * 0.09 +\n        practiceFit * 0.12 +\n        (1 - overload) * 0.18);\n    const retention = clamp(masteryGain * (0.55 + policy.retrievalPractice * 0.45));\n    const engagement = clamp(0.12 +\n        intuitionFit * 0.16 +\n        dialogueFit * 0.16 +\n        diagramFit * 0.1 +\n        reflectionFit * 0.14 +\n        (1 - overload) * 0.18);\n    const transfer = clamp(masteryGain * (0.55 + policy.interdisciplinaryBias * 0.25 + learner.transferDesire * 0.2));\n    const confusion = clamp(0.04 +\n        overload * 0.55 +\n        Math.abs(policy.formalism - learner.abstractionComfort) * 0.18 +\n        Math.abs(policy.challengeRate - learner.persistence) * 0.12);\n    const score = clamp(masteryGain * weights.masteryGain +\n        retention * weights.retention +\n        engagement * weights.engagement +\n        transfer * weights.transfer -\n        confusion * weights.confusion, 0, 1);\n    return {\n        learner,\n        topic,\n        masteryGain,\n        retention,\n        engagement,\n        transfer,\n        confusion,\n        score,\n        breakdown: {\n            intuitionFit,\n            rigorFit,\n            dialogueFit,\n            diagramFit,\n            practiceFit,\n            reflectionFit,\n            overload,\n        },\n        explanation: [\"Deterministic algebraic baseline.\"],\n    };\n}\n// ============================================================================\n// Prng (from src/core/random.ts)\n// ============================================================================\nclass Prng {\n    state;\n    constructor(seed) {\n        this.state = seed;\n    }\n    next() {\n        this.state = (this.state * 1103515245 + 12345) & 0x7fffffff;\n        return this.state / 0x7fffffff;\n    }\n    int(min, max) {\n        return Math.floor(min + this.next() * (max - min + 1));\n    }\n    bool() {\n        return this.next() >= 0.5;\n    }\n}\nexports.Prng = Prng;\n// ============================================================================\nexports.DEFAULT_POLICY = {\n    name: \"keating-default\",\n    analogyDensity: 0.72,\n    socraticRatio: 0.66,\n transfer: clamp(weights.transfer, 0.01, 1),\n        confusion: clamp(weights.confusion, 0.01, 1),\n    };\n    const sum = clamped.masteryGain +\n        clamped.retention +\n        clamped.engagement +\n        clamped.transfer +\n        clamped.confusion;\n    if (sum === 0)\n        return exports.DEFAULT_WEIGHTS;\n    return {\n        masteryGain: clamped.masteryGain / };\n}\nfunction clampPolicy(policy) {\n    return {\n        ...policy,\n        analogyDensity: clamp(policy.analogyDensity),\n        socraticRatio: clamp(policy.socraticRatio),\n        formalism: ============================================================================\nfunction prerequisiteBullets(topic) {\n    return topic.prerequisites.map((item) => `Recall ${item} and connect it to ${topic.title}.`);\n}\nfunction misconceptionBullets(topic) {\n    return topic.misconceptions.map((item) => `Address misconception: ${item}`);\n}\nfunction practiceBullets(topic, exerciseCount) {\n    const bullets = [...topic.exercises];\n    while (bullets.length < exerciseCount) {\n        bullets.push(`Invent a new example that makes ${topic.title} easier to explain.`);\n    }\n    return        id: \"orient\",\n            title: \"Orientation\",\n            purpose: \"Assess prerequisites and frame the core question.\",\n            bullets: [`State the big question: {\n            id: \"misconceptions\",\n            title: \"Misconception Repair\",\n            purpose: \"Anticipate predictable mistakes before they calcify.\",\n            bullets: (policy.diagramBias >= 0.55) {\n        phases.splice(4, 0, {\n            id: \"diagram\",\n            title: \"Diagram\",\n            purpose: \"Compress the concept into a visual structure before       phases[5].bullets.unshift(`Pause after each practice step and ask the learner to predict the next move.`);\n    }\n    if (topic.domain === \"code\") {\n        const exIdx =            purpose: \"Write and trace runnable code so the learner sees the concept execute.\",\n                bullets: [\n                    `Write a minimal runnable example demonstrating ${topic.title}.`,\n                    \"Step through execution line by line, narrating state changes.\",\n                    \"Ask the learner to predict output before running.\",\n                ],\n            });\n        }\n    }\n    return { topic, policy, phases };\n}\nfunction lessonPlanToMarkdown(plan) {\n    const lines = [\n        `# Lesson Plan: ${plan.topic.title}`,\n        \"\",\n        `- Domain: ${plan.topic.domain}`,\n        `- Policy: ${plan.policy.name}`,\n        `- Summary: ${plan.topic.summary}`,\n        \"\",\n    ];\n    for (const phase of plan.phases) {\n        lines.push(`## ${phase.title}`);\n        lines.push(phase.purpose);\n        lines.push(\"\");\n        for (const bullet of phase.bullets) {\n            lines.push(`- ${bullet}`);\n        }\n        lines.push(\"\");\n    }\n    return `${lines.join(\"\\n\").trim()}\\n`;\n}\n// ============================================================================\n// Concept Map Generation (from
