lessonPlans: await this.putMany(STORES.LESSON_PLANS, data.lessonPlans),\n            lessonMaps: await this.putMany(STORES.LESSON_MAPS, data.lessonMaps),\n            animations: await this.putMany(STORES.ANIMATIONS, data.animations),\n            verifications: await this.putMany(STORES.VERIFICATIONS, data.verifications),\n            benchmarks: await this.putMany(STORES.BENCHMARKS, data.benchmarks),\n            evolutions: await this.putMany(STORES.EVOLUTIONS, data.evolutions),\n            policies: await this.putMany(STORES.POLICIES, data.policies),\n            feedback: await this.putMany(STORES.FEEDBACK, data.feedback),\n            promptEvolutions: await this.putMany(STORES.PROMPT_EVOLUTIONS, data.promptEvolutions),\n            improvements: await this.putMany(STORES.IMPROVEMENTS, data.improvements),\n            goals: await this.putMany(STORES.GOALS, data.goals),\n            quizResults: await this.putMany(STORES.QUIZ_RESULTS, data.quizResults),\n            decks: await this.putMany(STORES.DECKS, data.decks),\n            cardReviews: await this.putMany(STORES.CARD_REVIEWS, data.cardReviews),\n            questionChecks: await this.putMany(STORES.QUESTION_CHECKS, data.questionChecks),\n        };\n        if (data.learnerState) {\n            const current = await this.getLearnerState();\n            const mergedFeedback = mergeFeedbackById(current.feedbackHistory, data.learnerState.feedbackHistory, data.feedback);\n            const bySessionId = new Map([...(current.sessions ?? []), ...(data.learnerState.sessions ?? [])].map((session) => [\n                session.id ?? `${session.startedAt}:${session.endedAt ?? \"open\"}`,\n                session,\n            ]));\n            const mergedState = this.normalizeLearnerState({\n                ...current,\n                ...data.learnerState,\n                topicsExplored: [...new Set([...(current.topicsExplored ?? []), ...(data.learnerState.strengths ?? [])])],\n                weaknesses: [...new Set([...(current.weaknesses ?? []), ...(data.learnerState.weaknesses ?? [])])],\n                sessions: [...bySessionId.values()],\n            });\n            await this.saveLearnerState(mergedState);\n        }\n        else if (data.feedback?.length) {\n            await this.getLearnerState();\n        }\n        await this.refreshDerivedLearnerProfile();\n        return result;\n    }\n    // Lesson Plans\n    async saveLessonPlan(topic, content, metadata) {\n        const plan = {\n            id: this.generateId(),\n            topic,\n            createdAt: Date.now(),\n            updatedAt: Date.now(),\n            content,\n            metadata,\n            sessionId: this.currentSessionId ?? { ...details.answers } : undefined,\n            partialCredits: details?.partialCredits ? { ...details.partialCredits } : undefined,\n            timing: details?.timing ? {\n                totalMs: details.timing.totalMs,\n                perQuestionMs: { ...details.timing.perQuestionMs },\n            } : undefined,\n            flaggedQuestionIds: details?.flaggedQuestionIds ? [...details.flaggedQuestionIds] : undefined,\n            pendingGradeQuestionIds: details?.pendingGradeQuestionIds ? [...details.pendingGradeQuestionIds] : undefined,\n            sessionId: this.currentSessionId ?? undefined,\n        };\n        await this.put(STORES.QUIZ_RESULTS, record);\n        await this.refreshDerivedLearnerProfile();\n        return record;\n    }\n    async saveQuizGrades(resultId, grades) {\n        const current = (await this.getQuizResults()).find((result) => result.id === resultId);\n        if (!current)\n            return null;\n        const byQuestionId = new Map((current.openEndedGrades ?? []).map((grade) => [grade.questionId, grade]));\n        for (const grade of grades)\n            byQuestionId.set(grade.questionId, { ...grade });\n        const gradedIds = new Set(grades.map((grade) => grade.questionId));\n        const updated = {\n            ...current,\n            openEndedGrades: [...byQuestionId.values()],\n            pendingGradeQuestionIds: (current.pendingGradeQuestionIds ?? []).filter((id) => !gradedIds.has(id)),\n        };\n        await this.put(STORES.QUIZ_RESULTS, updated);\n        await this.refreshDerivedLearnerProfile();\n        return updated;\n    }\n    async getQuizResults(topic) {\n        const results = topic\n            ? await this.getByTopic(STORES.QUIZ_RESULTS, topic)\n            : await this.getAll(STORES.QUIZ_RESULTS);\n        return results.map((record) => {\n            const current = { ...record };\n            delete current.confidence;\n            delete current.weightedScore;\n            return current;\n        });\n    }\n    async getTopicQuizStats(topic) {\n        const results = await this.getQuizResults(topic);\n        if (results.length < 5)\n            return null;\n        const sorted = [...results].sort((a, b) => b.score - a.score);\n        const avgScore = sorted.reduce((s, r) => s + r.score, 0) / sorted.length;\n        const partialCreditResults = sorted.filter((r) => typeof r.partialCreditPoints === \"number\");\n        const avgPartialCreditPoints = partialCreditResults.length > 0\n            ? partialCreditResults.reduce((sum, result) => sum + (result.partialCreditPoints ?? 0), 0) / partialCreditResults.length\n            : 0;\n        const qIdx = Math.floor(sorted.length * 0.25);\n        const topQuartile = sorted[qIdx]?.score ?? sorted[0]?.score ?? 0;\n        return { count: sorted.length, avgScore, avgPartialCreditPoints, topQuartile };\n    }\n    // Evolutions\n    async saveEvolution(bestScore, policy, report, topic, trace) {\n        const evolution = {\n            id: this.generateId(),\n            topic,\n            createdAt: Date.now(),\n            bestScore,\n            policy,\n            trace,\n            report,\n            sessionId: this.currentSessionId ?? undefined,\n        };\n        await this.put(STORES.EVOLUTIONS, evolution);\n        return evolution;\n    }\n    async getEvolutions(topic) {\n        if (topic) {\n            return this.getByTopic(STORES.EVOLUTIONS, topic);\n        }\n        return this.getAll(STORES.EVOLUTIONS);\n    }\n    // Policies\n    async savePolicy(content, active = true) {\n        // Deactivate other policies if this one is active\n        if (active) {\n            const policies = await this.getAll(STORES.POLICIES);\n            for (const p of policies) {\n                if (p.active) {\n                    p.active = false;\n                    p.updatedAt = Date.now();\n                    await this.put(STORES.POLICIES, p);\n                }\n            }\n        }\n        const policy = {\n            id: this.generateId(),\n            createdAt: Date.now(),\n            updatedAt: Date.now(),\n            content,\n            active,\n        };\n        await this.put(STORES.POLICIES, policy);\n        return policy;\n    }\n    async getActivePolicy() {\n        const policies = await this.getAll(STORES.POLICIES);\n        return policies.find((p) => p.active) || null;\n    }\n    async getPolicies() {\n        return this.getAll(STORES.POLICIES);\n    }\n    // Feedback\n    async recordFeedback(topic, signal, options = {}) {\n        const entry = {\n            id: this.generateId(),\n            topic,\n            signal,\n            createdAt: Date.now(),\n            source: options.source ?? \"explicit\",\n            evidence: options.evidence,\n            sessionId: options.sessionId ?? this.currentSessionId ?? undefined,\n            messageId: options.messageId,\n            referent: options.referent,\n            topicSource: options.topicSource ?? (options.source === \"turn-analysis\" ? \"turn-analysis\" : \"explicit\"),\n        };\n        await this.put(STORES.FEEDBACK, entry);\n        await this.updateLearnerState((state) => {\n            if (!state.feedbackHistory.some((existing) => existing.id === entry.id)) {\n                state.feedbackHistory.push(entry);\n            }\n            if (!state.topicsExplored.includes(topic)) {\n                state.topicsExplored.push(topic);\n            }\n            state.lastSessionAt = Date.now();\n        });\n        await this.refreshDerivedLearnerProfile();\n        return entry;\n    }\n    async recordLearnerTurnFeedback(messages) {\n        const state = await this.getLearnerState();\n        const fallbackTopic = state.topicsExplored.at(-1) ?? state.feedbackHistory.at(-1)?.topic ?? \"general\";\n        let count = 0;\n        const entries = [];\n        for (const message of messages) {\n            const role = message.role;\n            if (role !== \"user\" && role !== \"user-with-attachments\")\n                continue;\n            const text = browserMessageText(message);\n            const inferred = (0, core_1.inferBrowserLearnerTurnSignal)(text, fallbackTopic);\n            if (!inferred || inferred.topic === \"general\")\n                continue;\n            if (state.feedbackHistory.some((entry) => entry.source === \"turn-analysis\" && entry.evidence === inferred.evidence))\n                continue;\n            const entry = {\n                id: this.generateId(),\n                topic: inferred.topic,\n                signal: inferred.signal,\n                createdAt: Date.now(),\n                source: \"turn-analysis\",\n                evidence: inferred.evidence,\n            };\n            await this.put(STORES.FEEDBACK, entry);\n            entries.push(entry);\n            count += 1;\n        }\n        if (count > 0) {\n            await this.updateLearnerState((nextState) => {\n                for (const entry of entries) {\n                    if (!nextState.feedbackHistory.some((existing) => existing.id === entry.id)) {\n                        nextState.feedbackHistory.push(entry);\n                    }\n                    if (!nextState.topicsExplored.includes(entry.topic))\n                        nextState.topicsExplored.push(entry.topic);\n                }\n                nextState.lastSessionAt = Date.now();\n            });\n            await this.refreshDerivedLearnerProfile();\n        }\n        return count;\n    }\n    async getFeedback(topic) {\n        if (topic) {\n            return this.getByTopic(STORES.FEEDBACK, topic);\n        }\n        return this.getAll(STORES.FEEDBACK);\n    }\n    // Learner State\n    async getLearnerState() {\n        const state = await this.loadLearnerStateRecord();\n        const feedback = await this.getAll(STORES.FEEDBACK);\n        const merged = this.mergeFeedbackIntoLearnerState(state, feedback);\n        if (merged.changed) {\n            await this.saveLearnerState(merged.state);\n        }\n        return this.refreshDerivedLearnerProfile();\n    }\n    async saveLearnerState(state) {\n        const store = await this.getStore(STORES.LEARNER_STATE, \"readwrite\");\n        return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {\n            const request = store.put({\n                ...state,\n                schemaVersion: LEARNER_STATE_SCHEMA_VERSION,\n                id: \"learner-state\",\n            });\n            request.onsuccess = () => resolve();\n            request.onerror = () => reject(request.error);\n        });\n    }\n    async rememberLearnerProfileBelief(input) {\n        const value = input.value.trim().slice(0, 240);\n        const evidence = input.evidence.trim().slice(0, 500);\n        if (!value || !evidence)\n            throw new Error(\"A profile belief needs both a value and supporting evidence.\");\n        const now = Date.now();\n        let saved = null;\n        await this.updateLearnerState((state) => {\n            const normalized = value.toLocaleLowerCase();\n            const existing = state.profileBeliefs.find((belief) => belief.category === input.category && belief.value.toLocaleLowerCase() === normalized);\n            const requestedConfidence = Number.isFinite(input.confidence)\n                ? Math.max(0, Math.min(1, input.confidence))\n                : input.source === \"explicit\" ? 1 : 0.45;\n            const confidence = input.source === \"explicit\"\n                ? 1\n                : Math.min(0.65, requestedConfidence);\n            if (existing) {\n                existing.source = existing.source === \"explicit\" ? \"explicit\" : input.source;\n                existing.confidence = existing.source === \"explicit\"\n                    ? 1\n                    : Math.max(existing.confidence, confidence);\n                existing.evidence = evidence;\n                existing.updatedAt = now;\n                saved = { ...existing };\n                return;\n            }\n            const belief = {\n                id: this.generateId(),\n                category: input.category,\n                value,\n                source: input.source,\n                confidence,\n                evidence,\n                createdAt: now,\n                updatedAt: now,\n            };\n            state.profileBeliefs.push(belief);\n            saved = belief;\n        });\n        if (!saved)\n            throw new Error(\"Learner profile belief was not saved.\");\n        return saved;\n    }\n    async recordSessionStart() {\n        await this.updateLearnerState((state) => {\n            if (!state.sessions)\n                state.sessions = [];\n            const activeSessionId = this.currentSessionId;\n            const existing = activeSessionId\n                ? state.sessions.find((session) => session.id === activeSessionId && !session.endedAt)\n                : state.sessions.at(-1);\n            if (existing && !existing.endedAt) {\n                state.lastSessionAt = Date.now();\n                return;\n            }\n            state.sessions.push({\n                id: activeSessionId ?? undefined,\n                startedAt: Date.now(),\n                topicsCovered: [],\n            });\n            state.lastSessionAt = Date.now();\n        });\n    }\n    async recordSessionEnd(topicsCovered) {\n        const state = await this.getLearnerState();\n        if (!state.sessions)\n            state.sessions = [];\n        const current = state.sessions[state.sessions.length - 1];\n        if (current && !current.endedAt) {\n            current.endedAt = Date.now();\n            current.topicsCovered = topicsCovered;\n        }\n        await this.saveLearnerState(state);\n    }\n    // Prompt Evolutions\n    async savePromptEvolution(promptName, run) {\n        const result = {\n            id: this.generateId(),\n            promptName,\n            createdAt: Date.now(),\n            bestScore: run.bestScore,\n            bestPrompt: run.bestPrompt,\n            report: run.report,\n            sessionId: this.currentSessionId ?? undefined,\n        };\n        await this.put(STORES.PROMPT_EVOLUTIONS, result);\n        return result;\n    }\n    async getPromptEvolutions(promptName) {\n        if (promptName) {\n            return this.getByIndex(STORES.PROMPT_EVOLUTIONS, \"promptName\", promptName);\n        }\n        return this.getAll(STORES.PROMPT_EVOLUTIONS);\n    }\n    // Improvements\n    async saveImprovementAttempt(attempt) {\n        const record = {\n            id: this.generateId(),\n            createdAt: Date.now(),\n            ...attempt,\n            sessionId: this.currentSessionId ?? undefined,\n        };\n        await this.put(STORES.IMPROVEMENTS, record);\n        const archive = await this.getImprovementArchive();\n        archive.attempts.push({\n            proposal: {\n                id: attempt.proposalId,\n                timestamp: new Date().toISOString(),\n                targets: [],\n                hypothesis: attempt.hypothesis,\n                baselineScore: attempt.baselineScore,\n                status: attempt.accepted ? \"accepted\" : \"rejected\",\n            },\n            baselineScore: attempt.baselineScore,\n            afterScore: attempt.afterScore,\n            scoreDelta: attempt.scoreDelta,\n            accepted: attempt.accepted,\n            completedAt: new Date().toISOString(),\n        });\n        if (attempt.accepted) {\n            archive.totalAccepted += 1;\n            archive.cumulativeImprovement += attempt.scoreDelta ?? 0;\n        }\n        else {\n            archive.totalRejected += 1;\n        }\n        await this.saveImprovementArchive(archive);\n        return record;\n    }\n    async getImprovementAttempts() {\n        return this.getAll(STORES.IMPROVEMENTS);\n    }\n    async getImprovementArchive() {\n        const attempts = await this.getImprovementAttempts();\n        const archive = {\n            attempts: attempts.map((a) => ({\n                proposal: {\n                    id: a.proposalId,\n                    timestamp: new Date(a.createdAt).toISOString(),\n                    targets: a.targets.split(\",\").map((t) => ({ area: t.trim() })),\n                    hypothesis: a.hypothesis,\n                    baselineScore: a.baselineScore,\n                    status: a.accepted ? \"accepted\" : \"rejected\",\n                },\n                baselineScore: a.baselineScore,\n                afterScore: a.afterScore,\n                scoreDelta: a.scoreDelta,\n                accepted: a.accepted,\n                completedAt: new Date(a.createdAt).toISOString(),\n            })),\n            totalAccepted: attempts.filter((a) => a.accepted).length,\n            totalRejected: attempts.filter((a) => !a.accepted).length,\n            cumulativeImprovement: attempts.filter((a) => a.accepted).reduce((sum, a) => sum + (a.scoreDelta ?? 0), 0),\n        };\n        return archive;\n    }\n    async saveImprovementArchive(archive) {\n        const store = await this.getStore(STORES.LEARNER_STATE, \"readwrite\");\n        return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {\n            const request = store.put({ id: \"improvement-archive\", ...archive });\n            request.onsuccess = () => resolve();\n            request.onerror = () => reject(request.error);\n        });\n    }\n    // Flashcard Decks (SM-2 spaced repetition)\n    async saveDeck(deck) {\n        const now = Date.now();\n        const record = {\n            id: deck.id ?? this.generateId(),\n            topic: deck.topic,\n            slug: deck.slug,\n            title: deck.title,\n            description: deck.description,\n            cards: deck.cards,\n            createdAt: deck.createdAt ?? null;\n    }\n    async getDeckBySlug(slug) {\n        const decks = await this.getDecks();\n        return decks.find((d) => d.slug === slug) ?? null;\n    }\n    async updateDeckCardSrs(deckId, cardId, srs, updatedAt = Date.now()) {\n        const deck = await this.getDeck(deckId);\n        if (!deck)\n            return null;\n        let changed = false;\n        const cards = deck.cards.map((card) => {\n            if (card.id !== cardId)\n                return card;\n            changed = true;\n            return { ...card, srs, updatedAt };\n        });\n        if (!changed)\n            return deck;\n        const updated = { ...deck, cards, updatedAt };\n        await this.put(STORES.DECKS, updated);\n        return updated;\n    }\n    async deleteDeck(id) {\n        await this.deleteById(STORES.DECKS, id);\n    }\n    async recordCardReview(review) {\n        const record = {\n            id: review.id ?? this.generateId(),\n            deckId: review.deckId,\n            cardId: review.cardId,\n            topic: review.topic,\n            slug: review.slug,\n            rating: review.rating,\n            appliedIntervalDays: review.appliedIntervalDays,\n            easeAfter: review.easeAfter,\n            previousIntervalDays: review.previousIntervalDays,\n            isLapse: review.isLapse,\n            createdAt: review.createdAt ?? Math.max(0, Math.min(1, check.score)) : undefined,\n            grading: check.grading,\n            createdAt: check.createdAt ?? Date.now(),\n            sessionId: check.sessionId ?? this.currentSessionId ?? undefined,\n        };\n        await this.put(STORES.QUESTION_CHECKS, record);\n        await this.refreshDerivedLearnerProfile();\n        return record;\n    }\n    async getQuestionChecks(topic) {\n        if (topic)\n            return this.getByTopic(STORES.QUESTION_CHECKS, topic);\n        return this.getAll(STORES.QUESTION_CHECKS);\n    }\n    async gradeQuestionCheck(id, grade) {\n        const checks = await this.getQuestionChecks();\n        const current = checks.find((check) => check.id === id);\n        if (!current)\n            return null;\n        const updated = {\n            ...current,\n            score: Math.max(0, Math.min(1, grade.score)),\n            grading: \"model\",\n            misconception: grade.misconception?.trim() || undefined,\n        };\n        await this.put(STORES.QUESTION_CHECKS, updated);\n        await this.refreshDerivedLearnerProfile();\n        return updated;\n    }\n    // List all artifacts\n    async listArtifacts() {\n        const [plans, maps, animations, benchmarks, evolutions, promptEvos, improvements, decks] = await Promise.all([\n            this.getLessonPlans(),\n            this.getLessonMaps(),\n            this.getAnimations(),\n            this.getBenchmarks(),\n            this.getEvolutions(),\n            this.getPromptEvolutions(),\n            this.getImprovementAttempts(),\n            this.getDecks(),\n        ]);\n        return [\n            ...plans.map((p) => ({ id: p.id, label: `Plan: ${p.topic}`, type: \"plan\", createdAt: p.createdAt })),\n            ...maps.map((m) => ({ id: m.id, label: `Map: ${m.topic}`, type: \"map\", createdAt: m.createdAt })),\n            ...animations.map((a) => ({ id: a.id, label: `Animation: ${a.topic}`, type: \"animation\", createdAt: a.createdAt })),\n            ...decks.map((d) => ({ id: d.id, label: `Deck: ${d.title} (${d.cards.length} cards)`, type: \"deck\", createdAt: d.updatedAt })),\n            ...benchmarks.map((b) => ({\n                id: b.id,\n                label: `Benchmark: ${b.topic || \"general\"} (${b.score.toFixed(2)})`,\n                type: \"benchmark\",\n                createdAt: b.createdAt,\n            })),\n            ...evolutions.map((e) => ({\n                id: e.id,\n                label: `Evolution: ${e.topic || \"general\"} (${e.bestScore.toFixed(2)})`,\n                type: \"evolution\",\n                createdAt: e.createdAt,\n            })),\n            ...promptEvos.map((pe) => ({\n                id: pe.id,\n                label: `Prompt Evo: ${pe.promptName} (${pe.bestScore.toFixed(2)})`,\n                type: \"prompt-evolution\",\n                createdAt: pe.createdAt,\n            })),\n            ...improvements.map((imp) => ({\n                id: imp.id,\n                label: `Improve: ${imp.accepted ? \"✓\" : \"✗\"} ${imp.proposalId} (Δ${(imp.scoreDelta ?? 0) >= 0 ? \"+\" : \"\"}${(imp.scoreDelta ?? 0).toFixed(2)})`,\n                type: \"improvement\",\n                createdAt: imp.createdAt,\n            })),\n        ].sort((a, b) => b.createdAt - a.createdAt);\n    }\n}\nexports.KeatingStorage = KeatingStorage;\nfunction browserMessageText(message) {\n    const content = message.content;\n    if (typeof content === \"string\")\n        return content.trim();\n    if (Array.isArray(content)) {\n        return content\n            .map((item) => {\n            if (typeof item === \"string\")\n                return item;\n            if (item && typeof item === \"object\" && typeof item.text === \"string\") {\n                return item.text;\n            }\n            return \"\";\n        })\n            .filter(Boolean)\n            .join(\" \")\n            .trim();\n    }\n    return \"\";\n}\n// Default policy for browser\nexports.DEFAULT_BROWSER_POLICY = `# Keating Hyperteacher Policy\n\n## Teaching Approach\n- Diagnose before teaching\n- Guide with questions, not lectures\n- Encourage reconstruction over \"observed\";\n\t\tevidence: string;\n\t\tconfidence?: number;\n\t}): Promise<LearnerProfileBelief> {\n\t\tconst value = input.value.trim().slice(0, 240);\n\t\tconst evidence = LearnerProfileBelief | null = null;\n\t\tawait this.updateLearnerState((state) => {\n\t\t\tconst normalized = value.toLocaleLowerCase();\n\t\t\tconst existing = state.profileBeliefs.find((belief) Math.max(0, Math.min(1, input.confidence!))\n\t\t\t\t: input.source === \"explicit\" ? 1 : 0.45;\n\t\t\tconst confidence = input.source === \"explicit\"\n\t\t\t\t? 1\n\t\t\t\t: Math.min(0.65, requestedConfidence);\n\t\t\tif (existing) {\n\t\t\t\texisting.source = existing.source === \"explicit\" ?         request.onsuccess = () => resolve();\n            request.onerror = () => reject(request.error);\n        });\n    }\n    generateId() {\n        return [],\n            feedbackHistory: [],\n            strengths: [],\n            weaknesses: [],\n            topicProfiles: [],\n            sessionsCount: 0,\n            sessions: [],\n            profileBeliefs: [],\n            studyPriorities: [],\n        };\n    }\n    normalizeLearnerState(value) {\n        const raw = value && typeof value === \"object\" ? value : {};\n        return {\n            schemaVersion: LEARNER_STATE_SCHEMA_VERSION,\n            topicsExplored: Array.isArray(raw.topicsExplored) ? raw.topicsExplored.filter((topic) => typeof topic === \"string\") : [],\n            feedbackHistory: Array.isArray(raw.feedbackHistory) ? raw.feedbackHistory.filter((entry) => !!entry && typeof entry === \"object\") : [],\n            strengths: Array.isArray(raw.strengths) ? raw.strengths.filter((item) => typeof item === \"string\") : [],\n            weaknesses: Array.isArray(raw.weaknesses) ? raw.weaknesses.filter((item) => typeof item === \"string\") : [],\n            topicProfiles: Array.isArray(raw.topicProfiles)\n                ? raw.topicProfiles.filter((item) => !!item && typeof item === \"object\" && typeof item.topic === \"string\")\n                : [],\n            lastSessionAt: typeof raw.lastSessionAt === \"number\" ? raw.lastSessionAt : undefined,\n            sessionsCount: typeof raw.sessionsCount === \"number\" ? raw.sessionsCount : 0,\n            sessions: Array.isArray(raw.sessions) ? raw.sessions.filter((session) => !!session && typeof session === \"object\") : [],\n            profileBeliefs: Array.isArray(raw.profileBeliefs)\n                ? raw.profileBeliefs.filter((belief) => {\n                    if (!belief || typeof belief !== \"object\")\n                        return false;\n                    const candidate = belief;\n                    return typeof candidate.id === \"string\"\n                        && typeof candidate.value === \"string\"\n                        && typeof candidate.evidence === \"string\"\n                        && [\"motivation\", \"communication-preference\", \"learning-preference\", \"interest\"].includes(String(candidate.category));\n                })\n                : [],\n            studyPriorities: normalizeStudyPriorities(raw.studyPriorities),\n        };\n    }\n    mergeFeedbackIntoLearnerState(state, feedback) {\n        let changed = false;\n        const byId = new Set(state.feedbackHistory.map((entry) => entry.id));\n        for (const entry of feedback) {\n            if (!byId.has(entry.id)) {\n                state.feedbackHistory.push(entry);\n                byId.add(entry.id);\n                changed = true;\n            }\n        }\n        state.feedbackHistory.sort((a, b) => a.createdAt - b.createdAt);\n        const topics = new Set(state.topicsExplored);\n        for (const entry of state.feedbackHistory) {\n            if (entry.topic && !topics.has(entry.topic)) {\n                topics.add(entry.topic);\n                changed = true;\n            }\n        }\n        const nextTopics = [...topics];\n        if (nextTopics.length !== state.topicsExplored.length || nextTopics.some((topic, index) => topic !== state.topicsExplored[index])) {\n            state.topicsExplored = nextTopics;\n            changed = true;\n        }\n        if (state.sessionsCount !== state.sessions.length) {\n            state.sessionsCount = state.sessions.length;\n            changed = true;\n        }\n        return { state, changed };\n    }\n    async loadLearnerStateRecord() {\n        const store = await this.getStore(STORES.LEARNER_STATE);\n        return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {\n            const request = store.get(\"learner-state\");\n            request.onsuccess = () => resolve(this.normalizeLearnerState(request.result || this.defaultLearnerState()));\n            request.onerror = () => reject(request.error);\n        });\n    }\n    /** Rebuild the displayed learner profile from its durable evidence records. */\n    async refreshDerivedLearnerProfile() {\n        const [state, feedback, quizResults, questionChecks, cardReviews] = await Promise.all([\n            this.loadLearnerStateRecord(),\n            this.getAll(STORES.FEEDBACK),\n            this.getAll(STORES.QUIZ_RESULTS),\n            this.getAll(STORES.QUESTION_CHECKS),\n            this.getAll(STORES.CARD_REVIEWS),\n        ]);\n        const profile = (0, learner_profile_1.deriveLearnerProfile)([\n            ...feedback.map((entry) => ({\n                topic: entry.topic,\n                kind: \"feedback\",\n                score: entry.signal === \"thumbs-up\" ? 0.85 : entry.signal === \"confused\" ? 0.35 : 0.15,\n                createdAt: entry.createdAt,\n                weight: entry.source === \"explicit\" ? 0.25 : 0.1,\n                note: entry.evidence,\n            })),\n            ...quizResults\n                .filter((result) => result.totalQuestions > 0 || (result.openEndedGrades?.length ?? 0) > 0)\n                .map((result) => ({\n                topic: result.topic,\n                kind: \"quiz\",\n                score: quizEvidenceScore(result),\n                createdAt: result.createdAt,\n                weight: 1.2,\n            })),\n            ...questionChecks.filter((check) => typeof check.score === \"number\").map((check) => ({\n                topic: check.topic,\n                kind: \"diagnostic\",\n                score: check.score,\n                createdAt: check.createdAt,\n                weight: 0.8,\n                note: check.misconception,\n            })),\n            ...cardReviews.map((review) => ({\n                topic: review.topic,\n                kind: \"review\",\n                score: review.rating === 0 ? 0.05 : review.rating === 1 ? 0.45 : review.rating === 2 ? 0.75 : 0.9,\n                createdAt: review.createdAt,\n                weight: 0.3 + Math.min(0.7, Math.max(0, review.previousIntervalDays ?? 0) / 21),\n            })),\n        ]);\n        const topicsExplored = [...new Set([\n                ...state.topicsExplored,\n                ...profile.topics.map((topic) => topic.topic),\n            ])];\n        const next = {\n            ...state,\n            topicsExplored,\n            topicProfiles: profile.topics,\n            strengths: profile.strengths,\n            weaknesses: profile.weaknesses,\n        };\n        if (JSON.stringify(next) !== JSON.stringify(state))\n            await this.saveLearnerState(next);\n        return next;\n    }\n    async updateLearnerState(mutator) {\n        let updated = null;\n        const run = this.learnerStateWriteQueue.then(async () => {\n            const state = await this.getLearnerState();\n            await mutator(state);\n            state.sessionsCount = state.sessions.length;\n            await this.saveLearnerState(state);\n            updated = state;\n        });\n        this.learnerStateWriteQueue = run.catch(() => { });\n        await run;\n        return updated ?? this.getLearnerState();\n    }\n    // Learner Goals — long-horizon curricula, tracked across sessions\n    async saveGoal(goal) {\n        const record = {\n            ...goal,\n            sessionId: goal.sessionId ?? this.currentSessionId ?? undefined,\n            updatedAt: Date.now(),\n        };\n        await this.put(STORES.GOALS, record);\n        return record;\n    }\n    async getGoals() {\n        const goals = await this.getAll(STORES.GOALS);\n        return goals.sort((a, b) => b.updatedAt - a.updatedAt);\n    }\n    async getGoal(id) {\n        const goals = await this.getAll(STORES.GOALS);\n        return goals.find((g) => g.id === id) ?? null;\n    }\n    async deleteGoal(id) {\n        await this.deleteById(STORES.GOALS, id);\n    }\n    async exportPortableData() {\n        const [lessonPlans, lessonMaps, animations, verifications, benchmarks, evolutions, policies, feedback, learnerState, promptEvolutions, improvements, goals, quizResults, decks, cardReviews,] = await Promise.all([\n            this.getLessonPlans(),\n            this.getLessonMaps(),\n            this.getAnimations(),\n            this.getVerifications(),\n            this.getBenchmarks(),\n            this.getEvolutions(),\n            this.getPolicies(),\n this.getQuizResults(),\n            this.getDecks(),\n            this.getCardReviews(),\n        ]);\n        return {\n            lessonPlans,\n            lessonMaps,\n            animations,\n            verifications,\n            benchmarks,\n            evolutions,\n            policies,\n            feedback,\n            learnerState,\n            promptEvolutions,\n            improvements,\n            goals,\n            quizResults,\n            decks,\n            cardReviews,\n            questionChecks: await this.getQuestionChecks(),\n        };\n    }\n    async importPortableData(data) {\n        const result = {\n            lessonPlans: await this.putMany(STORES.LESSON_PLANS, data.lessonPlans),\n            lessonMaps: await policies: await this.putMany(STORES.POLICIES, data.policies),\n            feedback: await this.putMany(STORES.FEEDBACK, data.feedback),\n            promptEvolutions: await    cardReviews: await this.putMany(STORES.CARD_REVIEWS, data.cardReviews),\n            questionChecks: await this.putMany(STORES.QUESTION_CHECKS, data.questionChecks),\n        };\n        if undefined,\n        };\n        await this.put(STORES.BENCHMARKS, benchmark);\n        return benchmark;\n    }\n    async getBenchmarks(topic) {\n        if (topic) {\n            return this.getByTopic(STORES.BENCHMARKS, topic);\n        }\n        return this.getAll(STORES.BENCHMARKS);\n    }\n    // Quiz Results\n    async saveQuizResult(score, partialCreditPoints, totalQuestions, topic, details) {\n        const record = {\n            id: details?.resultId || this.generateId(),\n            topic: topic || \"general\",\n            createdAt: Date.now(),\n            score,\n            partialCreditPoints,\n            totalQuestions,\n            answers: details?.answers ? { ...details.answers } : undefined,\n            partialCredits: details?.partialCredits ? { ...details.partialCredits } : undefined,\n            timing: details?.timing ? {\n                totalMs: details.timing.totalMs,\n                perQuestionMs: { ...details.timing.perQuestionMs },\n            } : undefined,\n            flaggedQuestionIds: details?.flaggedQuestionIds ? [...details.flaggedQuestionIds] : undefined,\n            pendingGradeQuestionIds: details?.pendingGradeQuestionIds ? [...details.pendingGradeQuestionIds] : undefined,\n            sessionId: this.currentSessionId ?? undefined,\n        };\n        await this.put(STORES.QUIZ_RESULTS, record);\n        await this.refreshDerivedLearnerProfile();\n        return record;\n    }\n    async saveQuizGrades(resultId, grades) {\n        const current = (await this.getQuizResults()).find((result) => result.id === resultId);\n        if (!current)\n            return null;\n        const byQuestionId = new Map((current.openEndedGrades ?? []).map((grade) => [grade.questionId, grade]));\n        for (const grade of grades)\n            byQuestionId.set(grade.questionId, { ...grade });\n        const gradedIds = new Set(grades.map((grade) => grade.questionId));\n        const updated = {\n            ...current,\n            openEndedGrades: [...byQuestionId.values()],\n            pendingGradeQuestionIds: (current.pendingGradeQuestionIds ?? []).filter((id) => !gradedIds.has(id)),\n        };\n        await this.put(STORES.QUIZ_RESULTS, updated);\n        await this.refreshDerivedLearnerProfile();\n        return updated;\n    }\n    async getQuizResults(topic) {\n        const results = topic\n            ? await this.getByTopic(STORES.QUIZ_RESULTS, topic)\n            : await this.getAll(STORES.QUIZ_RESULTS);\n        return results.map((record) => {\n            const current = { ...record };\n            delete current.confidence;\n            delete current.weightedScore;\n            return current;\n        });\n    }\n    async getTopicQuizStats(topic) {\n        const results = await this.getQuizResults(topic);\n        if (results.length < 5)\n            return null;\n        const sorted = [...results].sort((a, b) => b.score - a.score);\n        const avgScore = sorted.reduce((s, r) => s + r.score, 0) / sorted.length;\n        const partialCreditResults = sorted.filter((r) => typeof r.partialCreditPoints === \"number\");\n        const avgPartialCreditPoints = partialCreditResults.length > 0\n            ? partialCreditResults.reduce((sum, result) => sum + (result.partialCreditPoints ?? 0), 0) / partialCreditResults.length\n            : 0;\n        const qIdx = Math.floor(sorted.length * 0.25);\n        const topQuartile = sorted[qIdx]?.score ?? sorted[0]?.score ?? 0;\n        return { count: sorted.length, avgScore, avgPartialCreditPoints, topQuartile };\n    }\n    // Evolutions\n    async saveEvolution(bestScore, policy, report, topic, trace) {\n        const evolution = {\n            id: this.generateId(),\n            topic,\n            createdAt: Date.now(),\n            bestScore,\n            policy,\n            trace,\n            report,\n            sessionId: this.currentSessionId ?? undefined,\n        };\n        await this.put(STORES.EVOLUTIONS, evolution);\n        return evolution;\n    }\n    async getEvolutions(topic) {\n        if (topic) {\n            return this.getByTopic(STORES.EVOLUTIONS, topic);\n        }\n        return this.getAll(STORES.EVOLUTIONS);\n    }\n    // Policies\n    async savePolicy(content, active = true) {\n        // Deactivate other policies if this one is active\n        if (active) {\n            const policies = await this.getAll(STORES.POLICIES);\n            for (const p of policies) {\n                if (p.active) {\n                    p.active = false;\n                    p.updatedAt = Date.now();\n                    await this.put(STORES.POLICIES, p);\n                }\n            }\n        }\n        const policy = {\n            id: this.generateId(),\n            createdAt: Date.now(),\n            updatedAt: Date.now(),\n            content,\n            active,\n        };\n        await this.put(STORES.POLICIES, policy);\n        return policy;\n    }\n    async getActivePolicy() {\n        const policies = await this.getAll(STORES.POLICIES);\n        return policies.find((p) => p.active) || null;\n    }\n    async getPolicies() {\n        return this.getAll(STORES.POLICIES);\n    }\n    // Feedback\n    async recordFeedback(topic, signal, options = {}) {\n        const entry = {\n            id: this.generateId(),\n            topic,\n            signal,\n            createdAt: Date.now(),\n            source: options.source ?? \"explicit\",\n            evidence: options.evidence,\n            sessionId: options.sessionId ?? this.currentSessionId ?? undefined,\n            messageId: options.messageId,\n            referent: options.referent,\n            topicSource: options.topicSource ?? (options.source === \"turn-analysis\" ? \"turn-analysis\" : \"explicit\"),\n        };\n        await this.put(STORES.FEEDBACK, entry);\n        await this.updateLearnerState((state) => {\n            if (!state.feedbackHistory.some((existing) => existing.id === entry.id)) {\n                state.feedbackHistory.push(entry);\n            }\n            if (!state.topicsExplored.includes(topic)) {\n                state.topicsExplored.push(topic);\n            }\n            state.lastSessionAt = Date.now();\n        });\n        await this.refreshDerivedLearnerProfile();\n        return entry;\n    }\n    async recordLearnerTurnFeedback(messages) {\n        const state = await this.getLearnerState();\n        const fallbackTopic = state.topicsExplored.at(-1) ?? state.feedbackHistory.at(-1)?.topic ?? \"general\";\n        let count = 0;\n        const entries = [];\n        for (const message of messages) {\n            const role = message.role;\n            if (role !== \"user\" && role !== \"user-with-attachments\")\n                continue;\n            const text = browserMessageText(message);\n            const inferred = (0, core_1.inferBrowserLearnerTurnSignal)(text, fallbackTopic);\n            if (!inferred || inferred.topic === \"general\")\n                continue;\n            if (state.feedbackHistory.some((entry) => entry.source === \"turn-analysis\" && entry.evidence === inferred.evidence))\n                continue;\n            const entry = {\n                id: this.generateId(),\n                topic: inferred.topic,\n                signal: inferred.signal,\n                createdAt: Date.now(),\n                source: \"turn-analysis\",\n                evidence: inferred.evidence,\n            };\n            await this.put(STORES.FEEDBACK, entry);\n            entries.push(entry);\n            count += 1;\n        }\n        if (count > 0) {\n            await this.updateLearnerState((nextState) => {\n                for (const entry of entries) {\n                    if (!nextState.feedbackHistory.some((existing) => existing.id === entry.id)) {\n                        nextState.feedbackHistory.push(entry);\n                    }\n                    if (!nextState.topicsExplored.includes(entry.topic))\n                        nextState.topicsExplored.push(entry.topic);\n                }\n                nextState.lastSessionAt = Date.now();\n            });\n            await this.refreshDerivedLearnerProfile();\n        }\n        return count;\n    }\n    async getFeedback(topic) {\n        if (topic) {\n            return this.getByTopic(STORES.FEEDBACK, topic);\n        }\n        return this.getAll(STORES.FEEDBACK);\n    }\n    // Learner State\n    async getLearnerState() {\n        const state = await this.loadLearnerStateRecord();\n        const feedback = await this.getAll(STORES.FEEDBACK);\n        const merged = this.mergeFeedbackIntoLearnerState(state, feedback);\n        if (merged.changed) {\n            await this.saveLearnerState(merged.state);\n        }\n        return this.refreshDerivedLearnerProfile();\n    }\n    async saveLearnerState(state) {\n        const store = await this.getStore(STORES.LEARNER_STATE, \"readwrite\");\n        return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {\n            const request = store.put({\n                ...state,\n                schemaVersion: LEARNER_STATE_SCHEMA_VERSION,\n                id: \"learner-state\",\n            });\n            request.onsuccess = () => resolve();\n            request.onerror = () => reject(request.error);\n        });\n    }\n    async setStudyPriority(input) {\n        return this.updateLearnerState((state) => {\n            state.studyPriorities = state.studyPriorities.filter((record) => record.targetId !== input.targetId || record.targetType !== input.targetType);\n            state.studyPriorities.push({ ...input, updatedAt: Date.now() });\n        });\n    }\n    async rememberLearnerProfileBelief(input) {\n        const value = input.value.trim().slice(0, 240);\n        const evidence = input.evidence.trim().slice(0, 500);\n        if (!value || !evidence)\n            throw new Error(\"A profile belief needs both a value and supporting evidence.\");\n        const now = Date.now();\n        let saved = null;\n        await this.updateLearnerState((state) => {\n            const normalized = value.toLocaleLowerCase();\n            const existing = state.profileBeliefs.find((belief) => belief.category === input.category && belief.value.toLocaleLowerCase() === normalized);\n            const requestedConfidence = Number.isFinite(input.confidence)\n                ? Math.max(0, Math.min(1, input.confidence))\n                : input.source === \"explicit\" ? 1 : 0.45;\n            const confidence = input.source === \"explicit\"\n                ? 1\n                : Math.min(0.65, requestedConfidence);\n            if (existing) {\n                existing.source = existing.source === \"explicit\" ? \"explicit\" : input.source;\n                existing.confidence = existing.source === \"explicit\"\n                    ? 1\n                    : Math.max(existing.confidence, confidence);\n                existing.evidence = evidence;\n                existing.updatedAt = now;\n                saved = { ...existing };\n                return;\n            }\n            const belief = {\n                id: this.generateId(),\n                category: input.category,\n                value,\n                source: input.source,\n                confidence,\n                evidence,\n                createdAt: now,\n                updatedAt: now,\n            };\n            state.profileBeliefs.push(belief);\n            saved = belief;\n        });\n        if (!saved)\n            throw new Error(\"Learner profile belief was not saved.\");\n        return saved;\n    }\n    async recordSessionStart() {\n        await this.updateLearnerState((state) => {\n            if (!state.sessions)\n                state.sessions = [];\n            const activeSessionId = this.currentSessionId;\n            const existing = activeSessionId\n                ? state.sessions.find((session) => session.id === activeSessionId && !session.endedAt)\n                : state.sessions.at(-1);\n            if (existing && !existing.endedAt) {\n                state.lastSessionAt = Date.now();\n                return;\n            }\n            state.sessions.push({\n                id: activeSessionId ?? undefined,\n                startedAt: Date.now(),\n                topicsCovered: [],\n            });\n            state.lastSessionAt = Date.now();\n        });\n    }\n    async recordSessionEnd(topicsCovered) {\n        const state = await this.getLearnerState();\n        if (!state.sessions)\n            state.sessions = [];\n        const current = state.sessions[state.sessions.length - 1];\n        if (current && !current.endedAt) {\n            current.endedAt = Date.now();\n            current.topicsCovered = topicsCovered;\n        }\n        await this.saveLearnerState(state);\n    }\n    // Prompt Evolutions\n    async savePromptEvolution(promptName, run) {\n        const result = {\n            id: this.generateId(),\n            promptName,\n            createdAt: Date.now(),\n            bestScore: run.bestScore,\n            bestPrompt: run.bestPrompt,\n            report: run.report,\n            sessionId: this.currentSessionId ?? undefined,\n        };\n        await this.put(STORES.PROMPT_EVOLUTIONS, result);\n        return result;\n    }\n    async getPromptEvolutions(promptName) {\n        if (promptName) {\n            return this.getByIndex(STORES.PROMPT_EVOLUTIONS, \"promptName\", promptName);\n        }\n        return this.getAll(STORES.PROMPT_EVOLUTIONS);\n    }\n    // Improvements\n    async saveImprovementAttempt(attempt) {\n        const record = {\n            id: this.generateId(),\n            createdAt: Date.now(),\n            ...attempt,\n            sessionId: this.currentSessionId ?? undefined,\n        };\n        await this.put(STORES.IMPROVEMENTS, record);\n        const archive = await this.getImprovementArchive();\n        archive.attempts.push({\n            proposal: {\n                id: attempt.proposalId,\n                timestamp: new Date().toISOString(),\n                targets: [],\n                hypothesis: attempt.hypothesis,\n                baselineScore: attempt.baselineScore,\n                status: attempt.accepted ? \"accepted\" : \"rejected\",\n            },\n            baselineScore: attempt.baselineScore,\n            afterScore: attempt.afterScore,\n            scoreDelta: attempt.scoreDelta,\n            accepted: attempt.accepted,\n            completedAt: new Date().toISOString(),\n        });\n        if (attempt.accepted) {\n            archive.totalAccepted += 1;\n            archive.cumulativeImprovement += attempt.scoreDelta ?? resolve(request.result);\n\t\t\trequest.onerror = () => reject(request.error);\n\t\t});\n\t}\n\n\tprivate async put<T>(storeName: string, data: T): Promise<string> {\n\t\tconst store = await string);\n\t\t\trequest.onerror = () => reject(request.error);\n\t\t});\n\t}\n\n\tprivate async putMany<T>(storeName: string, records: T[] | undefined): Promise<number> {\n\t\tif (!records?.length) return 0;\n\t\tfor (const record of records) {\n\t\t\tawait this.put(storeName, record);\n\t\t}\n\t\treturn records.length;\n\t}\n\n\tprivate async deleteById(storeName: string, id: string): Promise<void> {\n\t\tconst store = await this.getStore(storeName, \"readwrite\");\n\t\treturn new Promise((resolve, reject) => {\n\t\t\tconst request = store.delete(id);\n\t\t\trequest.onsuccess = () => resolve();\n\t\t\trequest.onerror = () => reject(request.error);\n\t\t});\n\t}\n\n\tprivate generateId(): string {\n\t\treturn `${Date.now()}-${Math.random().toString(36).substr(2, 9)}`;\n\t}\n\n\tprivate defaultLearnerState(): LearnerState {\n\t\treturn {\n\t\t\tschemaVersion: LEARNER_STATE_SCHEMA_VERSION,\n\t\t\ttopicsExplored: [],\n\t\t\tfeedbackHistory: [],\n\t\t\tstrengths: normalizeLearnerState(value: unknown): LearnerState {\n\t\tconst raw = value && typeof value === \"object\" ? value as Partial<LearnerState> : {};\n\t\treturn {\n\t\t\tschemaVersion: LEARNER_STATE_SCHEMA_VERSION,\n\t\t\ttopicsExplored: Array.isArray(raw.topicsExplored) ? raw.topicsExplored.filter((topic): topic is string => typeof topic === \"string\") : [],\n\t\t\tfeedbackHistory: Array.isArray(raw.feedbackHistory) ? raw.feedbackHistory.filter((entry): entry is FeedbackEntry => !!entry && typeof entry === \"object\") : [],\n\t\t\tstrengths: Array.isArray(raw.strengths) ? raw.strengths.filter((item): item is string => typeof item === \"string\") : [],\n\t\t\tweaknesses: Array.isArray(raw.weaknesses) ? raw.weaknesses.filter((item): item is string => typeof item === \"string\") : [],\n\t\t\ttopicProfiles: Array.isArray(raw.topicProfiles)\n\t\t\t\t? raw.topicProfiles.filter((item): item is LearnerTopicProfile => !!item && typeof item === \"object\" && typeof (item as LearnerTopicProfile).topic === \"string\")\n\t\t\t\t: [],\n\t\t\tlastSessionAt: typeof raw.lastSessionAt === \"number\" ? raw.lastSessionAt : undefined,\n\t\t\tsessionsCount: typeof raw.sessionsCount === \"number\" ? raw.sessionsCount : 0,\n\t\t\tsessions: Array.isArray(raw.sessions) ? raw.sessions.filter((session): session is is LearnerProfileBelief => {\n\t\t\t\t\tif (!belief || typeof belief !== \"object\") return false;\n\t\t\t\t\tconst candidate = belief as Partial<LearnerProfileBelief>;\n\t\t\t\t\treturn typeof candidate.id === \"string\"\n\t\t\t\t\t\t&& typeof candidate.value === \"string\"\n\t\t\t\t\t\t&& typeof candidate.evidence === \"string\"\n\t\t\t\t\t\t&& [\"motivation\", {\n\t\tlet changed = false;\n\t\tconst byId = new Set(state.feedbackHistory.map((entry) => entry.id));\n\t\tfor (const entry of feedback) {\n\t\t\tif (!byId.has(entry.id)) {\n\t\t\t\tstate.feedbackHistory.push(entry);\n\t\t\t\tbyId.add(entry.id);\n\t\t\t\tchanged = true;\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t}\n\t\tstate.feedbackHistory.sort((a, b) => a.createdAt - b.createdAt);\n\n\t\tconst topics = new Set(state.topicsExplored);\n\t\tfor (const entry of state.feedbackHistory) {\n\t\t\tif (entry.topic && !topics.has(entry.topic)) {\n\t\t\t\ttopics.add(entry.topic);\n\t\t\t\tchanged = true;\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t}\n\t\tconst nextTopics = [...topics];\n\t\tif (nextTopics.length !== state.topicsExplored.length || nextTopics.some((topic, index) => topic !== state.topicsExplored[index])) {\n\t\t\tstate.topicsExplored = nextTopics;\n\t\t\tchanged = true;\n\t\t}\n\n\t\tif (state.sessionsCount !== state.sessions.length) {\n\t\t\tstate.sessionsCount = state.sessions.length;\n\t\t\tchanged = true;\n\t\t}\n\n\t\treturn { state, changed };\n\t}\n\n\tprivate async loadLearnerStateRecord(): Promise<LearnerState> {\n\t\tconst store = await this.getStore(STORES.LEARNER_STATE);\n\t\treturn new this.defaultLearnerState()));\n\t\t\trequest.onerror = () => reject(request.error);\n\t\t});\n\t}\n\n\t/** Rebuild the displayed learner profile from its durable evidence records. */\n\tprivate async refreshDerivedLearnerProfile(): Promise<LearnerState> {\n\t\tconst [state, feedback, quizResults, questionChecks, cardReviews] = await Promise.all([\n\t\t\tthis.loadLearnerStateRecord(),\n\t\t\tthis.getAll<FeedbackEntry>(STORES.FEEDBACK),\n\t\t\tthis.getAll<QuizResultRecord>(STORES.QUIZ_RESULTS),\n\t\t\tthis.getAll<QuestionCheckRecord>(STORES.QUESTION_CHECKS),\n\t\t\tthis.getAll<CardReviewRecord>(STORES.CARD_REVIEWS),\n\t\t]);\n\t\tconst 1.2,\n\t\t\t\t})),\n\t\t\t...questionChecks.filter((check): check is QuestionCheckRecord & { score: number } => typeof check.score === \"number\").map((check) => ({\n\t\t\t\ttopic: === 1 ? 0.45 : review.rating === 2 ? 0.75 : 0.9,\n\t\t\t\tcreatedAt: review.createdAt,\n\t\t\t\tweight: 0.3 + Math.min(0.7, Math.max(0, review.previousIntervalDays ?? 0) / {\n\t\t\t...state,\n\t\t\ttopicsExplored,\n\t\t\ttopicProfiles: profile.topics,\n\t\t\tstrengths: profile.strengths,\n\t\t\tweaknesses: profile.weaknesses,\n\t\t};\n\t\tif (JSON.stringify(next) !== JSON.stringify(state)) await this.saveLearnerState(next);\n\t\treturn next;\n\t}\n\n\tprivate async updateLearnerState(mutator: (state: LearnerState) => void | Promise<void>): Promise<LearnerState> {\n\t\tlet updated: LearnerState | null = null;\n\t\tconst run = this.learnerStateWriteQueue.then(async () => {\n\t\t\tconst state = await this.getLearnerState();\n\t\t\tawait extractPartialJsonString(partialJson, field) {\n    const key = `\"${field}\"`;\n    const keyIndex = partialJson.indexOf(key);\n    if (keyIndex === -1)\n        return null;\n    // Advance past the   +function waitForDevicePoll(ms: number, signal?: AbortSignal): Promise<void> {
  +	return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
  +		if (signal?.aborted) {
  +			reject(new DOMException("The sign-in was cancelled.", "AbortError"));
  +			return;
  +		}
  +		const onAbort = () => {
  +			globalThis.clearTimeout(timeout);
  +			reject(new DOMException("The sign-in was cancelled.", "AbortError"));
  +		};
  +		const timeout = globalThis.setTimeout(() => {
  +			signal?.removeEventListener("abort", onAbort);
  +			resolve();
  +		}, ms);
  +		signal?.addEventListener("abort", onAbort, { once: true });
  +	});
  +}
  +
  +export async function completeOAuthDeviceFlow(
  +	provider: "github-copilot",
  +	signal?: AbortSignal,
  +): Promise<OAuthCallbackResult> {
  +	const pending = loadPendingOAuth();
  +	if (!pending || pending.flow !== "device-code" || pending.provider !== provider) {
  +		return { success: false, error: "No pending GitHub Copilot sign-in was found." };
  +	}
  +
  +	let intervalSeconds = pending.intervalSeconds;
  +	try {
  +		while (Date.now() < pending.expiresAt) {
  +			await waitForDevicePoll(intervalSeconds * 1000, signal);
  +			const response = await fetch("/api/oauth/github-copilot/poll", {
  +				method: "POST",
  +				headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
  +				body: JSON.stringify({ device_code: pending.deviceCode }),
  +			});
  +			if (response.status === 202) continue;
  +			if (response.status === 429) {
  +				intervalSeconds += 5;
  +				continue;
  +			}
  +			if (!response.ok) {
  +				throw new Error(`GitHub Copilot sign-in failed: ${response.status}`);
  +			}
  +			const tokens = await response.json();
  +			if (tokens.status !== "complete" || typeof tokens.access_token !== "string" || typeof tokens.refresh_token !== "string") {
  +				throw new Error("GitHub returned an invalid Copilot token response.");
  +			}
  +			await saveOAuthCredentials({
  +				refresh: tokens.refresh_token,
  +				access: tokens.access_token,
  +				expires: Date.now() + (tokens.expires_in ?? 3600) * 1000,
  +				provider,
  +			});
  +			clearPendingOAuth();
  +			return { success: true, provider };
  +		}
  +		clearPendingOAuth();
  +		return { success: false, error: "GitHub Copilot sign-in expired. Please try again." };
  +	} catch (error) {
  +		return {
  +			success: false,
  +			error: error instanceof Error ? error.message : "GitHub Copilot sign-in failed.",
  +		};
  +	}
  +}

  +	studyPriorities: StudyPriorityRecord[];

  +export type StudyPriority = "focus" | "maintain" | "low";
  +export type StudyPriorityTarget = "deck" | "verification" | "topic";
  +
  +export interface StudyPriorityRecord {
  +	targetId: string;
  +	targetType: StudyPriorityTarget;
  +	priority: StudyPriority;
  +	updatedAt: number;
  +}
  +
  +const STUDY_PRIORITIES: StudyPriority[] = ["focus", "maintain", "low"];
  +const STUDY_PRIORITY_TARGETS: StudyPriorityTarget[] = ["deck", "verification", "topic"];
  +
  +export function normalizeStudyPriorities(value: unknown): StudyPriorityRecord[] {
  +	if (!Array.isArray(value)) return [];
  +	const byTarget = new Map<string, StudyPriorityRecord>();
  +	for (const item of value) {
  +		if (!item || typeof item !== "object") continue;
  +		const candidate = item as Partial<StudyPriorityRecord>;
  +		if (
  +			typeof candidate.targetId !== "string"
  +			|| !candidate.targetId.trim()
  +			|| !STUDY_PRIORITY_TARGETS.includes(candidate.targetType as StudyPriorityTarget)
  +			|| !STUDY_PRIORITIES.includes(candidate.priority as StudyPriority)
  +			|| typeof candidate.updatedAt !== "number"
  +			|| !Number.isFinite(candidate.updatedAt)
  +		) continue;
  +		const normalized: StudyPriorityRecord = {
  +			targetId: candidate.targetId,
  +			targetType: candidate.targetType as StudyPriorityTarget,
  +			priority: candidate.priority as StudyPriority,
  +			updatedAt: candidate.updatedAt,
  +		};
  +		const key = `${normalized.targetType}:${normalized.targetId}`;
  +		const current = byTarget.get(key);
  +		if (!current || normalized.updatedAt >= current.updatedAt) byTarget.set(key, normalized);
  +	}
  +	return [...byTarget.values()].sort((left, right) => left.updatedAt - right.updatedAt);
  +}
  +
  +export function mergeStudyPriorities(
  +	...lists: Array<StudyPriorityRecord[] | undefined>
  +): StudyPriorityRecord[] {
  +	return normalizeStudyPriorities(lists.flatMap((list) => list ?? []));
   }

  +describe("GitHub Copilot device OAuth", () => {
  +	it("starts a trusted github.com device authorization", async () => {
  +		let requestBody = "";
  +		const device = await startGitHubCopilotDeviceFlow((async (input, init) => {
  +			expect(String(input)).toBe("https://github.com/login/device/code");
  +			requestBody = String(init?.body ?? "");
  +			return Response.json({
  +				device_code: "device-secret",
  +				user_code: "ABCD-EFGH",
  +				verification_uri: "https://github.com/login/device",
  +				interval: 5,
  +				expires_in: 900,
  +			});
  +		}) as typeof fetch);
  +
  +		expect(new URLSearchParams(requestBody).get("scope")).toBe("read:user");
  +		expect(device).toEqual({
  +			device_code: "device-secret",
  +			user_code: "ABCD-EFGH",
  +			verification_uri: "https://github.com/login/device",
  +			interval: 5,
  +			expires_in: 900,
  +		});
  +	});
  +
  +	it("reports authorization_pending without exposing an upstream token", async () => {
  +		const result = await pollGitHubCopilotDeviceFlow("device-secret", (async () =>
  +			Response.json({ error: "authorization_pending" })) as unknown as typeof fetch);
  +		expect(result).toEqual({ status: "pending" });
  +	});
  +
  +	it("exchanges an approved device code for a refreshable Copilot credential", async () => {
  +		const urls: string[] = [];
  +		const fetcher = (async (input, init) => {
  +			urls.push(String(input));
  +			if (urls.length === 1) {
  +				expect(new URLSearchParams(String(init?.body)).get("device_code")).toBe("device-secret");
  +				return Response.json({ access_token: "github-access" });
  +			}
  +			expect(new Headers(init?.headers).get("authorization")).toBe("Bearer github-access");
  +			return Response.json({ token: "copilot-access", expires_at: Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000) + 1800 });
  +		}) as typeof fetch;
  +
  +		const result = await pollGitHubCopilotDeviceFlow("device-secret", fetcher);
  +		expect(urls).toEqual([
  +			"https://github.com/login/oauth/access_token",
  +			"https://api.github.com/copilot_internal/v2/token",
  +		]);
  +		expect(result.status).toBe("complete");
  +		if (result.status === "complete") {
  +			expect(result.access_token).toBe("copilot-access");
  +			expect(result.refresh_token).toBe("github-access");
  +			expect(result.expires_in).toBeGreaterThan(1400);
  +		}
  +	});
  +
  +	it("refreshes through the Nitro route using the durable GitHub token", async () => {
  +		globalThis.fetch = (async (_input, init) => {
  +			expect(new Headers(init?.headers).get("authorization")).toBe("Bearer github-access");
  +			return Response.json({ token: "copilot-refreshed", expires_at: Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000) + 1800 });
  +		}) as typeof fetch;
  +		const handler = (await import("../../server/api/oauth/refresh")).default;
  +		const event = mockEvent(
  +			new Request("https://keating.test/api/oauth/refresh", {
  +				method: "POST",
  +				headers: { "content-type": "application/json" },
  +				body: JSON.stringify({ provider: "github-copilot", refresh_token: "github-access" }),
  +			}),
  +		);
  +		const result = await handler(event);
  +		expect(result.status).toBe("complete");
  +		expect(result.access_token).toBe("copilot-refreshed");
  +	});
  +
  +	it("supports direct refresh helper coverage for a production smoke harness", async () => {
  +		const result = await refreshGitHubCopilotToken(
  +			"github-access",
  +			(async () => Response.json({ token: "copilot-refreshed", expires_at: Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000) + 1800 })) as unknown as typeof fetch,
  +		);
  +		expect(result.status).toBe("complete");
  +	});
  +
  +	it("preserves a GitHub credential when refresh fails transiently", async () => {
  +		globalThis.fetch = (async () => new Response("unavailable", { status: 503 })) as unknown as typeof fetch;
  +		const handler = (await import("../../server/api/oauth/refresh")).default;
  +		const event = mockEvent(
  +			new Request("https://keating.test/api/oauth/refresh", {
  +				method: "POST",
  +				headers: { "content-type": "application/json" },
  +				body: JSON.stringify({ provider: "github-copilot", refresh_token: "github-access" }),
  +			}),
  +		);
  +		try {
  +			await handler(event);
  +			throw new Error("Expected refresh to fail");
  +		} catch (error) {
  +			expect((error as { statusCode?: number }).statusCode).toBe(502);
  +		}
  +	});
  +});

  +# Development
  +
  +How to work in the Keating repo: the devenv environment and its task runner.
  +
  +## Environment
  +
  +All development dependencies (bun, node, typst, similarity, etc.) are managed by **devenv** (`devenv.nix`). Enter the dev environment with:
  +
  +```bash
  +devenv shell
  +```
  +
  +`devenv shell` also installs repo-local git hooks (see [Git hooks](#git-hooks)) and runs `keating:install` on first entry when dependencies are missing.
  +
  +## Tasks
  +
  +Every build, test, and release workflow is a **devenv task**, namespaced with `keating:` so task names never collide with devenv's built-ins or other projects.
  +
  +```bash
  +devenv tasks list          # List all available tasks
  +devenv tasks run keating:test
  +devenv tasks run keating:web
  +```
  +
  +Tasks that accept arguments pass them through to the CLI, e.g. `devenv tasks run keating:bench linear-algebra`.
  +
  +See the [devenv tasks documentation](https://devenv.sh/tasks/) for how tasks work, running tasks with arguments, dependencies, and the task runner.
  +
  +### Install / bootstrap
  +
  +| Task | Description |
  +|------|-------------|
  +| `keating:install` | `bun install` for root + web workspaces |
  +
  +### Version sync
  +
  +| Task | Description |
  +|------|-------------|
  +| `keating:bumpy` | Run bumpy (version bumper). Passes through args. |
  +| `keating:bump-version` | Bump version and sync across manifests (e.g. `keating:bump-version version`) |
  +| `keating:sync-version` | Sync version numbers across all manifests and source files |
  +| `keating:check-version` | Verify all version strings are in sync (CI-friendly) |
  +
  +### Build
  +
  +| Task | Description |
  +|------|-------------|
  +| `keating:build` | Build root TypeScript project (requires versions in sync) |
  +| `keating:generate-nodepod-boot` | Generate NodePod boot files from source tree |
  +| `keating:build-all` | Sync versions, build root, generate nodepod boot, build web |
  +
  +### Test
  +
  +| Task | Description |
  +|------|-------------|
  +| `keating:test` | Run the root test suite (`bun test ./test/*.test.ts`) |
  +| `keating:test-web` | Run the web test suite |
  +| `keating:test-e2e` | Real Pi RPC + tool-loop smoke test (requires `KEATING_E2E=1` and secrets) |
  +| `keating:mutate` | Mutation testing with Stryker against `src/core/` |
  +
  +### Mobile (Expo / React Native)
  +
  +| Task | Description |
  +|------|-------------|
  +| `keating:mobile` | Build, install, and launch the native Expo dev client on Android |
  +| `keating:mobile-start` | Start Metro for the installed native dev client |
  +| `keating:mobile-prebuild` | Generate the Android native project from Expo configuration |
  +| `keating:mobile-prebuild-clean` | Regenerate the Android native project (clean) |
  +| `keating:mobile-apk` | Build a locally installable Android debug APK |
  +| `keating:mobile-check` | Typecheck + test the React Native app |
  +| `keating:mobile-export` | Produce a production Android JS bundle locally |
  +
  +### Web
  +
  +| Task | Description |
  +|------|-------------|
  +| `keating:web` | Start the Keating web UI dev server (Vite on port 3000) |
  +| `keating:web-build` | Build the Keating web UI for production (vite + nitro) |
  +| `keating:web-preview` | Build and preview the Keating web UI production build |
  +| `keating:storybook` | Launch the Storybook component explorer |
  +
  +### CLI shortcuts
  +
  +| Task | Description |
  +|------|-------------|
  +| `keating:shell` | Launch the hyperteacher shell |
  +| `keating:doctor` | Run the hyperteacher doctor |
  +| `keating:bench` | Run benchmarks (e.g. `keating:bench linear-algebra`) |
  +| `keating:evolve` | Evolve the teaching policy (e.g. `keating:evolve linear-algebra`) |
  +| `keating:prompt-evolve` | Evolve a prompt template (default: learn) |
  +| `keating:plan` | Generate a lesson plan for a topic |
  +| `keating:map` | Generate a lesson map for a topic |
  +| `keating:verify` | Generate a fact-checking checklist before teaching |
  +| `keating:animate` | Animate a teaching artifact for a topic |
  +| `keating:trace` | Trace a teaching session (filter by substring) |
  +
  +### Video
  +
  +| Task | Description |
  +|------|-------------|
  +| `keating:video-intro` | Render the narrated Keating intro video |
  +| `keating:video-web-stitch` | Stitch captured web UI frames into `docs/assets/web-*.mp4` |
  +
  +## Git hooks
  +
  +Repo-local git hooks are configured via devenv and call `devenv tasks run` directly — no separate task runner needed:
  +
  +- `pre-commit`: `keating:check-version`
  +- `pre-push`: `keating:test` + `keating:test-web`

  diff --git a/web/server/api/oauth/github-copilot-device.ts b/web/server/api/oauth/github-copilot-device.ts
  new file mode 100644
  index 0000000..f3d6adb
  --- /dev/null

  @@ -0,0 +1,17 @@
  +import { createError, defineEventHandler } from "h3";
  +import { startGitHubCopilotDeviceFlow } from "./github-copilot";
  +
  +export default defineEventHandler(async () => {
  +	try {
  +		return await startGitHubCopilotDeviceFlow();
  +	} catch (error) {
  +		console.error(
  +			"[oauth/github-copilot/device] Device authorization failed:",
  +			error instanceof Error ? error.message : "Unknown error",
  +		);
  +		throw createError({
  +			statusCode: 502,
  +			statusMessage: "GitHub device authorization failed",
  +		});
  +	}
  +});

  diff --git a/web/server/api/oauth/github-copilot-poll.ts b/web/server/api/oauth/github-copilot-poll.ts
  new file mode 100644
  index 0000000..c56f2e0
  --- /dev/null
  +++ b/web/server/api/oauth/github-copilot-poll.ts
  @@ -0,0 +1,39 @@
  +import { createError, defineEventHandler, readBody, setResponseStatus } from "h3";
  +import { pollGitHubCopilotDeviceFlow } from "./github-copilot";
  +
  +interface PollRequestBody {
  +	device_code?: string;
  +}
  +
  +export default defineEventHandler(async (event) => {
  +	const body = await readBody<PollRequestBody>(event);
  +	if (!body?.device_code) {
  +		throw createError({ statusCode: 400, statusMessage: "Missing device_code" });
  +	}
  +
  +	try {
  +		const result = await pollGitHubCopilotDeviceFlow(body.device_code);
  +		if (result.status === "pending") {
  +			setResponseStatus(event, 202);
  +			return result;
  +		}
  +		if (result.status === "slow_down") {
  +			setResponseStatus(event, 429);
  +			return result;
  +		}
  +		if (result.status === "failed") {
  +			throw createError({ statusCode: 400, statusMessage: result.error });
  +		}
  +		return result;
  +	} catch (error) {
  +		if ((error as { statusCode?: number }).statusCode) throw error;
  +		console.error(
  +			"[oauth/github-copilot/poll] Device token exchange failed:",
  +			error instanceof Error ? error.message : "Unknown error",
  +		);
  +		throw createError({
  +			statusCode: 502,
  +			statusMessage: "GitHub Copilot token exchange failed",
  +		});
  +	}
  +});

  diff --git a/web/server/api/oauth/github-copilot.ts b/web/server/api/oauth/github-copilot.ts
  new file mode 100644
  index 0000000..ab6238d
  --- /dev/null
  +++ b/web/server/api/oauth/github-copilot.ts
  @@ -0,0 +1,152 @@
  +const GITHUB_CLIENT_ID = "Iv1.b507a08c87ecfe98";
  +const GITHUB_DEVICE_CODE_URL = "https://github.com/login/device/code";
  +const GITHUB_ACCESS_TOKEN_URL = "https://github.com/login/oauth/access_token";
  +const GITHUB_COPILOT_TOKEN_URL = "https://api.github.com/copilot_internal/v2/token";
  +
  +const COPILOT_HEADERS = {
  +	"User-Agent": "GitHubCopilotChat/0.35.0",
  +	"Editor-Version": "vscode/1.107.0",
  +	"Editor-Plugin-Version": "copilot-chat/0.35.0",
  +	"Copilot-Integration-Id": "vscode-chat",
  +};
  +
  +export class OAuthUpstreamError extends Error {
  +	constructor(
  +		message: string,
  +		readonly status: number,
  +	) {
  +		super(message);
  +		this.name = "OAuthUpstreamError";
  +	}
  +}
  +
  +export interface GitHubCopilotDeviceAuthorization {
  +	device_code: string;
  +	user_code: string;
  +	verification_uri: string;
  +	interval: number;
  +	expires_in: number;
  +}
  +
  +export type GitHubCopilotDevicePollResult =
  +	| { status: "pending" }
  +	| { status: "slow_down" }
  +	| { status: "failed"; error: string }
  +	| {
  +			status: "complete";
  +			access_token: string;
  +			refresh_token: string;
  +			expires_in: number;
  +		};
  +
  +function requireRecord(value: unknown, message: string): Record<string, unknown> {
  +	if (!value || typeof value !== "object") throw new Error(message);
  +	return value as Record<string, unknown>;
  +}
  +
  +async function readJson(response: Response, message: string): Promise<Record<string, unknown>> {
  +	if (!response.ok) throw new OAuthUpstreamError(`${message}: upstream returned ${response.status}`, response.status);
  +	return requireRecord(await response.json(), message);
  +}
  +
  +export async function startGitHubCopilotDeviceFlow(
  +	fetcher: typeof fetch = fetch,
  +): Promise<GitHubCopilotDeviceAuthorization> {
  +	const response = await fetcher(GITHUB_DEVICE_CODE_URL, {
  +		method: "POST",
  +		headers: {
  +			Accept: "application/json",
  +			"Content-Type": "application/x-www-form-urlencoded",
  +			"User-Agent": COPILOT_HEADERS["User-Agent"],
  +		},
  +		body: new URLSearchParams({
  +			client_id: GITHUB_CLIENT_ID,
  +			scope: "read:user",
  +		}),
  +	});
  +	const data = await readJson(response, "GitHub device authorization failed");
  +	const verificationUri = typeof data.verification_uri === "string" ? data.verification_uri : "";
  +	let parsedVerificationUri: URL;
  +	try {
  +		parsedVerificationUri = new URL(verificationUri);
  +	} catch {
  +		throw new Error("GitHub returned an invalid device verification URL");
  +	}
  +	if (parsedVerificationUri.protocol !== "https:" || parsedVerificationUri.hostname !== "github.com") {
  +		throw new Error("GitHub returned an untrusted device verification URL");
  +	}
  +	if (
  +		typeof data.device_code !== "string" ||
  +		typeof data.user_code !== "string" ||
  +		typeof data.expires_in !== "number"
  +	) {
  +		throw new Error("GitHub returned an invalid device authorization response");
  +	}
  +	return {
  +		device_code: data.device_code,
  +		user_code: data.user_code,
  +		verification_uri: parsedVerificationUri.href,
  +		interval: typeof data.interval === "number" ? data.interval : 5,
  +		expires_in: data.expires_in,
  +	};
  +}
  +
  +async function exchangeGitHubCopilotToken(
  +	githubAccessToken: string,
  +	fetcher: typeof fetch,
  +): Promise<Extract<GitHubCopilotDevicePollResult, { status: "complete" }>> {
  +	const response = await fetcher(GITHUB_COPILOT_TOKEN_URL, {
  +		headers: {
  +			Accept: "application/json",
  +			Authorization: `Bearer ${githubAccessToken}`,
  +			...COPILOT_HEADERS,
  +		},
  +	});
  +	const data = await readJson(response, "GitHub Copilot token exchange failed");
  +	if (typeof data.token !== "string" || typeof data.expires_at !== "number") {
  +		throw new Error("GitHub returned an invalid Copilot token response");
  +	}
  +	return {
  +		status: "complete",
  +		access_token: data.token,
  +		refresh_token: githubAccessToken,
  +		expires_in: Math.max(1, Math.floor(data.expires_at - Date.now() / 1000 - 300)),
  +	};
  +}
  +
  +export async function pollGitHubCopilotDeviceFlow(
  +	deviceCode: string,
  +	fetcher: typeof fetch = fetch,
  +): Promise<GitHubCopilotDevicePollResult> {
  +	const response = await fetcher(GITHUB_ACCESS_TOKEN_URL, {
  +		method: "POST",
  +		headers: {
  +			Accept: "application/json",
  +			"Content-Type": "application/x-www-form-urlencoded",
  +			"User-Agent": COPILOT_HEADERS["User-Agent"],
  +		},
  +		body: new URLSearchParams({
  +			client_id: GITHUB_CLIENT_ID,
  +			device_code: deviceCode,
  +			grant_type: "urn:ietf:params:oauth:grant-type:device_code",
  +		}),
  +	});
  +	const data = await readJson(response, "GitHub device token polling failed");
  +	if (typeof data.access_token === "string") {
  +		return exchangeGitHubCopilotToken(data.access_token, fetcher);
  +	}
  +	if (data.error === "authorization_pending") return { status: "pending" };
  +	if (data.error === "slow_down") return { status: "slow_down" };
  +	const description = typeof data.error_description === "string" ? data.error_description : undefined;
  +	return {
  +		status: "failed",
  +		error: description ?? (typeof data.error === "string" ? data.error : "GitHub device authorization failed"),
  +	};
  +}
  +
  +export async function refreshGitHubCopilotToken(
  +	githubAccessToken: string,
  +	fetcher: typeof fetch = fetch,
  +): Promise<Extract<GitHubCopilotDevicePollResult, { status: "complete" }>> {
  +	return exchangeGitHubCopilotToken(githubAccessToken, fetcher);
  +}

  diff --git a/web/src/components/ImageGenerationRetryButton.stories.tsx b/web/src/components/ImageGenerationRetryButton.stories.tsx
  new file mode 100644
  index 0000000..9ef88ba
  --- /dev/null
  +++ b/web/src/components/ImageGenerationRetryButton.stories.tsx
  @@ -0,0 +1,24 @@
  +import type { Meta, StoryObj } from "@storybook/react-vite";
  +import { fn } from "storybook/test";
  +import { css } from "../../styled-system/css";
  +import { ImageGenerationRetryButton } from "./ImageGenerationRetryButton";
  +
  +const meta = {
  +	title: "Models/ImageGenerationRetryButton",
  +	component: ImageGenerationRetryButton,
  +	parameters: { layout: "centered" },
  +	decorators: [
  +		(Story) => (
  +			<div className={css({ borderRadius: "0.375rem", border: "1px solid color-mix(in srgb, var(--destructive) 60%, transparent)", backgroundColor: "color-mix(in srgb, var(--destructive) 10%, transparent)", padding: "0.75rem", fontSize: "0.75rem", color: "var(--destructive)" })}>
  +				<p>Image generation failed before the provider returned an image.</p>
  +				<Story />
  +			</div>
  +		),
  +	],
  +	args: { onRetry: fn() },
  +} satisfies Meta<typeof ImageGenerationRetryButton>;
  +
  +export default meta;
  +type Story = StoryObj<typeof meta>;
  +

  diff --git a/web/src/components/MultiSelectDropdown.tsx b/web/src/components/MultiSelectDropdown.tsx
  new file mode 100644
  index 0000000..613b23d
  --- /dev/null
  +++ b/web/src/components/MultiSelectDropdown.tsx
